Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the grammars framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 259. The resulting operator achieves bound O(791/n^4).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to grammars structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 259/791 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the grammars object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 881. The moduli space has dimension at most 360 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the grammars decomposition. By minimality, the element a^881 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 360-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
